RN/MSA Coverage 8 teams

Find the team for the consult type, then the RN covering the patient’s last-name initial. Each pair shares their group’s consults — the initials show each RN’s primary letters, but both RNs cover everything in their group.

    ICT PACT Primary Care

    ICT PACT Primary Care: ICTs are an extension of the provider. Though they can help in all areas, such as scheduling like an MSA, working up patients like an LPN, doing foot exams and case managing like an RN, they can also submit consults and orders like x-rays, MRI, and labs per provider request. Their main job is to help the provider with the workload they encounter and help them not be so overwhelmed. They help bridge a gap, for example by assisting in assessing patients if providers are running behind, scribing for the provider, and assisting in procedures (i.e. suture removals). They are certified in BFA and have their own clinic to help close gaps. Basically, ICTs are jacks of all trades, but their main purpose is to help providers not be overwhelmed with the workload and not take over nurses’ responsibilities.

    JCBOC ICTs
